```text
START -> TSK-1122
TSK-1122 -> TSK-1120
TSK-1120 -> TSK-1121
TSK-1120 -> TSK-1098
TSK-1121 -> TSK-1098
```

Notes:

- Split with PR 449 (TSK-1109 to TSK-1114, the design audit of
  2026-10-31; that PR's branch is not edited here). PR 449 owns the visual
  system: the root scale (TSK-1109), the palette (TSK-1110), the figure
  frame chrome, keyed legends, rich Details and the disclosure summary
  `chips` field (TSK-1111), stage at-rules (TSK-1112), the `stats`,
  titled callout, labelled `code` and chips blocks (TSK-1113) and the
  portal frame (TSK-1114). This PR owns the information structure: the
  view tree and shell (TSK-1120), topic reuse and change marks
  (TSK-1121), the design approval (TSK-1122), the status facts and
  document (TSK-1095, TSK-1098, TSK-1099), the encoded table (TSK-1096),
  the bar (TSK-1097) and the schedule family (TSK-1100). Where both touch
  one file (`document.rs`, `render.rs`, `chrome.tsx`, `styles.css`,
  `figure-grammar.mjs`) the records name one writer at a time; TSK-1109
  lands before TSK-1120 where both are ready because the shell is laid
  out on the root scale (an ordering note, not a graph edge, since that
  record is on PR 449's branch and `validate --docs` refuses an edge to a
  task this branch does not hold);
  TSK-1096 draws its summary mark and count through TSK-1111's `chips`
  field where landed and adds a field of the same shape otherwise;
  TSK-1098 uses TSK-1113's `stats` and labelled `code` where landed and
  falls back to extent rows and a plain `code` block otherwise; TSK-1112
  lands before TSK-1101 where both are ready. Nothing is planned twice:
  no PR 449 task draws a side menu, breadcrumb, view tree, topic or
  change mark, and no task here draws a block kind, token or frame
  chrome.
- Order and estimate, agentic, from the planned-throughput rule and this
  line's observed review and landing cycle of about an hour: TSK-1122
  runs first on the design seat, about two lane-hours to the operator's
  walk-through, which is the operator's time and undated; TSK-1120 starts
  when TSK-1122 has landed and, where ready together, after TSK-1109
  (PR 449); TSK-1121 follows
  TSK-1120; TSK-1098 now waits on TSK-1121 beside its four earlier
  predecessors, so the status document lands about three lane-hours later
  than the Progress report amendment estimated under the favourable
  scenario, about five under planning, and the adverse scenario adds the
  operator's walk-through wait. None of these is a calendar commitment.
- The approval of TSK-1122 is the operator's and is not a process rule
  added to every unit: it is one design pass for one new surface, as the
  map's "settle product, UX, UI or visual direction" row already requires,
  made clickable on real data because the operator asked for that form.
- The 13:15 direction (outcomes first, every outcome drilled into) is the
  layout contract's primary structure in TSK-1120 and the view tree in
  TSK-1098, not a view option; TSK-1122's prototype shows it first.
- Both lineages are to discover TSK-1120 to TSK-1122 independently at
  dispatch, as the Progress report amendment's tasks were; this amendment
  records the Claude planner's shape, and the Codex review of this PR is
  the other lineage's read of it. Codex round 8 at `d5d3ec8e9`
  (2026-10-31, 17:41 to 17:49 UTC) confirmed the operator's direction is
  carried and requested five changes, folded in: the shell's budgets are
  per view with a 1024-view, 16384-block document budget so a 300-task
  report keeps one view per task (TSK-1120 AC-4, AC-8; TSK-1098 AC-10);
  a view's change mark covers its title, kind, context, membership and
  placement, with new and removed views (TSK-1121 AC-3); the shell,
  topic and session revisions are scoped to present documents and a
  portal page keeps its source and route identity (TSK-1097 item j);
  the missing-topic and registry-carrier checks have owners (TSK-1121
  AC-8, TSK-1120 AC-4) and TSK-1097 keeps only the guidance; an export
  height is reused only under the rendering contract it was measured
  under, so PR 449's scale and recomposition changes invalidate earlier
  measurements (TSK-1101). Nits fixed: the topic example and the
  lower-case normalisation rule, `present feedback --format v2` with the
  acknowledge loop in TSK-1099, and the amended-task count here.
  TSK-1122 cites the first dashboard prototype the operator is reviewing.
  Round 9 at `897bf8772` confirmed those fixes and found one more, folded
  in: the raised block budget was not reconciled with the stored
  revision's read limit (`state.rs:4196`, `:1250`, `limits.rs:63`), so
  TSK-1120 now owns the pre-write serialised-size check that refuses a
  revision before any session state changes, with near-boundary and
  over-storage-bound fixtures (TSK-1120 AC-9).

## Design decisions amendment (2026-10-31)

The operator gave design decisions directly on 2026-10-31 while
reviewing the status dashboard prototype, recorded with their times in
the coordinator's handoff, section 5: the design direction of about
14:58 to 15:0x and the rows of 16:35, 18:37, 18:4x and 18:5x. The
typing row of 2026-10-27 in the same section (fully typed by default,
TypeScript for new files) is the source for types. The 18:5x row on
visual explanation and the layered portal also holds the operator's
words verbatim, quoted in the table. Only what these sources
say is credited to the operator; the rest is labelled as the plan's
elaboration. This amendment turns each decision into
a testable criterion in an existing task of this PR. No task is added.
One edge is added: graph v35 gains `TSK-1097 -> TSK-1120`, because the
shell's display popover offers the themes, modes and accents of
TSK-1097's token system and reuses its fit test. AC-190, AC-192 and
AC-195 are amended to name the decisions.

The design reference is the design seat's working prototype
`dashboard-v2`, a session scratchpad that is not committed. Its token
build (with WCAG, CIEDE2000 and dichromacy math) and its browser tests
(smoke, menu, states, fit, axe) show how each criterion can be checked.
It is not shipped code; TSK-1122 commits the approved prototype.

| Decision | Source | Criterion |
|---|---|---|
| Mode (light, dark, system) is separate from Themes | operator, about 14:58 to 15:0x | TSK-1097 AC-10; TSK-1120 AC-11 |
| Graphite is the default theme and the neutral one; no separate Neutral theme | operator, about 14:58 to 15:0x | TSK-1097 AC-10 |
| Graphite canvas: soft off-white and near-black, true greys with no cast | operator, about 14:58 to 15:0x | TSK-1097 AC-10 |
| Graphite's default accent is clear blue; each theme offers at least two curated accents the viewer selects | operator, 18:37, refining the 14:58 "restrained accent" | TSK-1097 AC-10 |
| One token system serves `cf-present` and the docs portal | operator, about 14:58 to 15:0x, and 18:37 (a portal surface on the same tokens) | TSK-1097 AC-9 |
| Pills sit on one line | operator, about 14:58 to 15:0x ("pills sit on one line"); the half-pixel row height test is the plan's measurement | TSK-1097 AC-17 |
| The menu is resizable, collapses to a rail and peeks | operator, about 14:58 to 15:0x | TSK-1120 AC-13 |
| One collapse and expand control, a panel-left icon | operator, 18:37, refining the 14:58 menu clause | TSK-1120 AC-10 |
| Motion is smooth and tested on recorded frames | operator, about 14:58 to 15:0x; transform and opacity only and reduced motion are the plan's reading of "smooth" | TSK-1120 AC-12, AC-14 |
| `cf-present` is a generic surface; the report template is preferred, not rigid | operator, about 14:58 to 15:0x | TSK-1097 AC-18 |
| One gear opens a display popover, Mode first | operator, 18:5x | TSK-1120 AC-11; portal version TSK-1097 AC-10 |
| No thick left-border callouts | operator, 18:5x | TSK-1097 AC-11 |
| Stage colours meet contrast, CIEDE2000 and colour-deficiency floors, with a minimum segment width and ring arc | operator, 18:4x | TSK-1097 AC-12 |
| Entity pages open with what the item is about, then status, decisions and blockers, no filler | operator, 18:4x | TSK-1098 AC-13; TSK-1095 AC-15 (the `about` fact) |
| The side menu is browser-tested | operator, 16:35 ("browser-tested side menu"); the list of checks is the plan's | TSK-1120 AC-12 |
| No text overflow | operator, 16:35 ("no text overflow"); the fit test and the ring-label rule are the plan's | TSK-1097 AC-13; TSK-1120 AC-12 |
| Typed code | operator, the typing row of 2026-10-27 (fully typed by default, TypeScript for new files); tests and values declared once in one modular source are the plan's elaboration | TSK-1097 AC-9; TSK-1120 AC-12 |
| Reports explain visually, with a considered range of colours per topic | operator, 18:5x (verbatim: "cf-present should opt to explain things visually", "not just rely on texts in pretty boxes", "use a range of colors as needed for the topic") | TSK-1097 AC-14 |
| The docs portal is visually rich and layered | operator, 18:5x (verbatim: "visually rich way - and in layered way of presenting information", "each layer again should present things visually") | TSK-446 AC-10 |
| Samples of every report scenario and portal layer, checked for their main figure | operator, 18:5x; the coverage test is the plan's elaboration of the 18:5x row | TSK-1097 AC-16 |
| Never Mermaid | workspace `AGENTS.md` ("Visuals first") and `.codeflow/rules/writing.md` | TSK-1097 AC-15 |
| The prototype shows these decisions and the added report scenarios | operator, 18:37 | TSK-1122 AC-5 |

- The plan's elaborations, labelled as such in the criteria: tokens
  declared once in one modular source, the typed token module and the
  ban on colour, size and radius literals (TSK-1097 AC-9), the canvas
  lightness bands (AC-10), the fit test and the ring-label rule (AC-13),
  inline SVG from the tokens with an accessible name and the Mermaid
  refusal (AC-15), the coverage test (AC-16), the half-pixel pill height
  test (AC-17), and the browser checks, the transform and opacity limit
  and reduced motion that make the menu and motion clauses testable
  (TSK-1120 AC-12 to AC-14).
- Refinements: the 18:37 single panel-left toggle refines the 14:58 menu
  clause (one control in place of two; resize, rail and peek stay); clear
  blue and the curated accent set refine the 14:58 "restrained accent".
  No 14:58 clause is retired.
- Feature pages: CodeFlow has no feature record apart from capabilities
  and outcomes, so a feature maps to its capability entry in
  `docs/capabilities.md` (`CAP-NNN`), which an epic lists in its
  `capabilities` field. The page stays because the operator named it, and
  the capability entry is the record that says what the feature is.
- Floors and minimums: the 24-degree accent distance from the status
  hues comes from the prototype's token build. The CIEDE2000 and
  dichromacy floors, the minimum segment width, the minimum ring arc,
  the canvas lightness bands and the menu width bounds are declared once
  in the token source, and TSK-1097's builder names them in its AC-1
  findings. These are reversible choices for the review to check.
- Report scenarios: TSK-1097 ships the status report, epic close,
  security review, weekly workspace digest and design review as present
  specimens, so the coverage check has a fixed set to run over.
- Order: TSK-1120 now waits on TSK-1097 as well as TSK-1122. TSK-1122
  waits on the operator's walk-through and TSK-1097 on TSK-1096, so in
  the favourable case the added edge moves nothing, and in the adverse
  case the shell starts about one lane-hour later.
- Numbering: this PR lands after PR 449, which holds AC-181 to AC-189
  and graph v34, so this PR's criteria are AC-190 to AC-196 and its
  graph is v35.

## CI merged completions amendment (2026-10-31)

Two issues filed against 3.1.1 on 2026-10-31 had no open task. Two tasks
join this epic, AC-197 and AC-198 serve them, and graph v36 adds
`TSK-1083 -> TSK-1123`, `TSK-1066 -> TSK-1124` and
`TSK-1124 -> TSK-1123`; no existing edge changes. This amendment takes
the numbers after pull request 449 (AC-181 to AC-189, graph v34) and
pull request 438 (AC-190 to AC-196, graph v35, tasks to TSK-1122); it
lands after both, or renumbers on merge.

| Task | Change | Source | Criteria |
|---|---|---|---|
| TSK-1123 (new) | A completion that reaches a task range unchanged through a merge is not bound at the range head, so later commits of the task under review are not blamed on it, and two completions in one range do not refuse each other | issue #455 and its reporter's comment | AC-1 to AC-7; serves AC-197 |
| TSK-1124 (new) | A sync that takes a `main` task's record unchanged, after `main` reopened and recompleted it, is not a change the line makes; status steps stay judged | issue #456 | AC-1 to AC-6; serves AC-198 |

- TSK-1066, TSK-1124 and TSK-1123 form one chain, and TSK-1083 also
  precedes TSK-1123, because each edits the same region of
  `workgraph/acceptance.rs`; one writer at a time. TSK-1036 (status
  steps a sync carries) is not an edge: issue #456 concerns criteria,
  and TSK-1124 leaves status-step judgment as TSK-1036 AC-7 sets it.
- Both bind trust (which completions and records a range accepts without
  re-judging), so each design gets a Fable review and each build a
  cross-lineage review with the `cf-security-reviewer` checklist.
- Issue #456 also reports that a completed task's own branch cannot push
  its completion (the family of #454). TSK-350 AC-13 covers the
  `work.criteria_frozen` shape of #454; the refusal to start from a
  complete record is still uncovered, and TSK-1124 AC-1 asks the builder
  to name what it needs for the primary to file.
